  context_label: Unique Spells
  template: =UNIQUE(FILTER(AA{r}:AA205,AA{r}:AA205<>"Empty"))
  note: Original Google Sheets formula recovered from DUMMYFUNCTION wrapper. Functions like FILTER, QUERY, UNIQUE, FLATTEN, IMPORTRANGE, GOOGLETRANSLATE are Sheets-only and must be reimplemented.
  e.g. AL2: =UNIQUE(FILTER(AA2:AA205,AA2:AA205<>"Empty"))
